   }\n        let tags = self\n            .sql(&format!(\n                \"SELECT hex(tag) FROM monster_tags WHERE monster_slug = {} ORDER BY position;\",\n                sql_text(&slug)\n            ))?\n            .lines()\n            .map(decode_sql_text)\n            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;\n        Ok(Some(Monster {\n            slug,\n            name,\n            cr,\n            armor_class,\n            hit_points,\n            tags,\n        }))\n    }\n\n    fn create_item(&self, item: &Item) -> Result<bool, ()> {\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM items WHERE slug = {};\",\n            sql_text(&item.slug)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO items(slug,name,type,rarity,cost_gp) VALUES ({},{},{},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(&item.slug),\n            sql_text(&item.name),\n            sql_text(&item.kind),\n            sql_text(&item.rarity),\n            item.cost_gp\n        ))\n        .map(|_| true)\n    }\n\n    fn item(&self, slug: &str) -> Result<Option<Item>, ()> {\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(slug),hex(name),hex(type),hex(rarity),cost_gp FROM items WHERE slug = {};\",\n            sql_text(slug)\n        ))?;\n        let Some(fields) = row.trim().split_once('|') else {\n            return Ok(None);\n        };\n        let mut fields = std::iter::once(fields.0).chain(fields.1.split('|'));\n        let slug = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n        let name = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n        let kind = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n        let rarity = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n        let cost_gp = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if fields.next().is_some() {\n            return Err(());\n        }\n        Ok(Some(Item {\n            slug,\n            name,\n            kind,\n            rarity,\n            cost_gp,\n        }))\n    }\n\n    /// Combat and authentication state predate the relational tables, so it is\n    /// stored together in one versioned BLOB.  Its deterministic encoding is\n    /// part of the persistence contract and must remain backward compatible.\n    fn load(&self) -> Result<(Sessions, Users), ()> {\n        let value = self.sql(\"SELECT hex(data) FROM game_state WHERE id = 1;\")?;\n        let value = value.trim();\n        if value.is_empty() {\n            return Ok((HashMap::new(), HashMap::new()));\n        }\n        decode_state(&decode_hex(value)?).ok_or(())\n    }\n\n    fn save(&self, sessions: &Sessions, users: &Users) -> Result<(), ()> {\n        let bytes = encode_state(sessions, users);\n        let statement = format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO game_state(id, data) VALUES (1, X'{}') ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET data = excluded.data;\",\n            hex(&bytes)\n        );\n        self.sql(&statement).map(|_| ())\n    }\n\n    fn sql(&self, statement: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n        let output = Command::new(\"sqlite3\")\n            .arg(&self.path)\n            .arg(statement)\n            .output()\n            .map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if output.status.success() {\n            String::from_utf8(output.stdout).map_err(|_| ())\n        } else {\n            Err(())\n        }\n    }\n\n}\n\nfn sql_text(value: &str) -> String {\n    format!(\"CAST(X'{}' AS TEXT)\", hex(value.as_bytes()))\n}\nfn decode_sql_text(value: &str) -> Result<String, ()> {\n    String::from_utf8(decode_hex(value)?).map_err(|_| ())\n}\n\nfn put_u64(out: &mut Vec<u8>, value: u64) {\n    out.extend_from_slice(&value.to_be_bytes());\n}\nfn put_i64(out: &mut Vec<u8>, value: i64) {\n    out.extend_from_slice(&value.to_be_bytes());\n}\nfn put_string(out: &mut Vec<u8>, value: &str) {\n    put_u64(out, value.len() as u64);\n    out.extend_from_slice(value.as_bytes());\n}\n\nfn encode_state(sessions: &Sessions, users: &Users) -> Vec<u8> {\n    let mut out = STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC_V2.to_vec();\n    let mut usernames = users.keys().collect::<Vec<_>>();\n    usernames.sort();\n    put_u64(&mut out, usernames.len() as u64);\n    for username in usernames {\n        put_string(&mut out, username);\n        put_string(&mut out, &users[username].password_hash);\n        put_string(&mut out, &users[username].role);\n    }\n    let mut ids = sessions.keys().collect::<Vec<_>>();\n    ids.sort();\n    put_u64(&mut out, ids.len() as u64);\n    for id in ids {\n        let session = &sessions[id];\n        put_string(&mut out, id);\n        put_i64(&mut out, session.round);\n        put_u64(&mut out, session.turn_index as u64);\n        put_u64(&mut out, session.order.len() as u64);\n        for combatant in &session.order {\n            put_string(&mut out, &combatant.name);\n            put_i64(&mut out, combatant.dex);\n            put_i64(&mut out, combatant.score);\n            out.push(u8::from(combatant.has_condition_bucket));\n            put_u64(&mut out, combatant.conditions.len() as u64);\n            for condition in &combatant.conditions {\n                put_string(&mut out, &condition.condition);\n                put_i64(&mut out, condition.remaining_rounds);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    out\n}\n\nstruct StateReader<'a> {\n    bytes: &'a [u8],\n    at: usize,\n}\nimpl<'a> StateReader<'a> {\n    fn bytes(&mut self, count: usize) -> Option<&'a [u8]> {\n        let end = self.at.checked_add(count)?;\n        let value = self.bytes.get(self.at..end)?;\n        self.at = end;\n        Some(value)\n    }\n    fn u64(&mut self) -> Option<u64> {\n        Some(u64::from_be_bytes(self.bytes(8)?.try_into().ok()?))\n    }\n    fn i64(&mut self) -> Option<i64> {\n        Some(i64::from_be_bytes(self.bytes(8)?.try_into().ok()?))\n    }\n    fn string(&mut self) -> Option<String> {\n        let count = usize::try_from(self.u64()?).ok()?;\n        String::from_utf8(self.bytes(count)?.to_vec()).ok()\n    }\n}\n\nfn decode_state(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<(Sessions, Users)> {\n    let mut r = StateReader { bytes, at: 0 };\n    let version = r.bytes(STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC.len())?;\n    if version != STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC && version != STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC_V2 {\n        return None;\n    }\n    let mut users = HashMap::new();\n    for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n        let name = r.string()?;\n        let password_hash = r.string()?;\n        // Version 1 did not retain a role. Treat such accounts as players so\n        // old persisted state cannot accidentally gain DM authority.\n        let role = if version == STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC_V2 {\n            r.string()?\n        } else {\n            \"player\".to_string()\n        };\n        users.insert(\n            name,\n            User {\n                password_hash,\n                role,\n            },\n        );\n    }\n    let mut sessions = HashMap::new();\n    for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n        let id = r.string()?;\n        let round = r.i64()?;\n        let turn_index = usize::try_from(r.u64()?).ok()?;\n        let mut order = Vec::new();\n        for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n            let name = r.string()?;\n            let dex = r.i64()?;\n            let score = r.i64()?;\n            let has_condition_bucket = r.bytes(1)?[0] != 0;\n            let mut conditions = Vec::new();\n            for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n                conditions.push(Condition {\n                    condition: r.string()?,\n                    remaining_rounds: r.i64()?,\n                });\n            }\n            order.push(Combatant {\n                name,\n                dex,\n                score,\n                conditions,\n                has_condition_bucket,\n            });\n        }\n        if order.is_empty() || turn_index >= order.len() {\n            return None;\n        }\n        sessions.insert(\n            id,\n            CombatSession {\n                round,\n                turn_index,\n                order,\n            },\n        );\n    }\n    (r.at == bytes.len()).then_some((sessions, users))\n}\n\nfn hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {\n    bytes.iter().map(|byte| format!(\"{byte:02X}\")).collect()\n}\nfn decode_hex(text: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, ()> {\n    if text.len() % 2 != 0 {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    (0..text.len())\n        .step_by(2)\n        .map(|at| u8::from_str_radix(&text[at..at + 2], 16).map_err(|_| ()))\n        .collect()\n}\n\nfn main() -> std::io::Result<()> {\n    let port = env::var(\"PORT\").unwrap_or_else(|_| DEFAULT_PORT.to_string());\n    let listener = TcpListener::bind(format!(\"127.0.0.1:{port}\"))?;\n    let mut storage = Storage::open(DATABASE_PATH)?;\n    let (mut sessions, mut users) = storage.load().unwrap_or_default();\n    for mut stream in listener.incoming().flatten() {\n        let _ = handle(&mut stream, &mut sessions, &mut users, &mut storage);\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Route one fully read request. self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        if campaign.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayInventoryItems::UnknownCampaign);\n        }\n        let member = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\n            sql_text(actor)\n        ))?;\n        if member.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayInventoryItems::Forbidden);\n        }\n        let character = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\n            sql_text(character_id)\n        ))?;\n        if character.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayInventoryItems::Missing);\n        }\n        let items = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(item_id),quantity FROM play_campaign_character_inventory_items WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {} ORDER BY item_id;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(character_id)\n        ))?.lines().map(|line| {\n            let mut fields = line.split('|');\n            let item_id = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            let quantity = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n            if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n            Ok((item_id, quantity))\n        }).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, ()>>()?;\n        Ok(PlayInventoryItems::Found(items))\n    }\n\n    fn remove_play_inventory_item(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        actor: &str,\n        character_id: &str,\n        item_id: &str,\n        quantity: i64,\n    ) -> Result<PlayInventoryItems, ()> {\n        match self.play_character_owner(campaign_id, actor, character_id)? {\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Found { owner } if owner == actor => {}\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::UnknownCampaign => {\n                return Ok(PlayInventoryItems::UnknownCampaign)\n            }\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Missing => return Ok(PlayInventoryItems::Missing),\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Found { .. } | PlayCharacterOwnership::Forbidden => {\n                return Ok(PlayInventoryItems::Forbidden)\n            }\n            _ => return Err(()),\n        }\n        let held = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT quantity FROM play_campaign_character_inventory_items WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {} AND item_id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(character_id), sql_text(item_id)\n        ))?;\n        let held: i64 = if held.trim().is_empty() {\n            0\n        } else {\n            held.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?\n        };\n        if quantity > held {\n            return Ok(PlayInventoryItems::Insufficient);\n        }\n        let total_quantity = held - quantity;\n        if total_quantity == 0 {\n            self.sql(&format!(\n                \"DELETE FROM play_campaign_character_inventory_items WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {} AND item_id = {};\",\n                sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(character_id), sql_text(item_id)\n            ))?;\n        } else {\n            self.sql(&format!(\n                \"UPDATE play_campaign_character_inventory_items SET quantity = {} WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {} AND item_id = {};\",\n                total_quantity, sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(character_id), sql_text(item_id)\n            ))?;\n        }\n        Ok(PlayInventoryItems::Updated { total_quantity })\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_loot(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, loot_id: &str, item_id: &str, quantity: i64) -> Result<PlayLootResult, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayLootResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlayLootResult::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n    let mut users = HashMap::new();\n    for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n        let name = r.string()?;\n        let password_hash = r.string()?;\n        // Version 1 did not retain a role. Treat such accounts as players so\n        // old persisted state cannot accidentally gain DM authority.\n        let role = if version == STATE_FORMAT_MAGIC_V2 {\n            r.string()?\n        } else {\n            \"player\".to_string()\n        };\n        users.insert(\n            name,\n            User {\n                password_hash,\n                role,\n            },\n        );\n    }\n    let mut sessions = HashMap::new();\n    for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n        let id = r.string()?;\n        let round = r.i64()?;\n        let turn_index = usize::try_from(r.u64()?).ok()?;\n        let mut order = Vec::new();\n        for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n            let name = r.string()?;\n            let dex = r.i64()?;\n            let score = r.i64()?;\n            let has_condition_bucket = r.bytes(1)?[0] != 0;\n            let mut conditions = Vec::new();\n            for _ in 0..r.u64()? {\n                conditions.push(Condition {\n                    condition: r.string()?,\n                    remaining_rounds: r.i64()?,\n                });\n            }\n            order.push(Combatant {\n                name,\n                dex,\n                score,\n                conditions,\n                has_condition_bucket,\n            });\n        }\n        if order.is_empty() || turn_index >= order.len() {\n            return None;\n        }\n        sessions.insert(\n            id,\n            CombatSession {\n                round,\n                turn_index,\n                order,\n            },\n        );\n    }\n    (r.at == bytes.len()).then_some((sessions, users))\n}\n\nfn hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {\n    bytes.iter().map(|byte| format!(\"{byte:02X}\")).collect()\n}\nfn decode_hex(text: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, ()> {\n    if text.len() % 2 != 0 {\n        return Err(());\n    }\n    (0..text.len())\n        .step_by(2)\n        .map(|at| u8::from_str_radix(&text[at..at + 2], 16).map_err(|_| ()))\n        .collect()\n}\n\nfn main() -> std::io::Result<()> {\n    let port = env::var(\"PORT\").unwrap_or_else(|_| DEFAULT_PORT.to_string());\n    let listener = TcpListener::bind(format!(\"127.0.0.1:{port}\"))?;\n    let mut storage = Storage::open(DATABASE_PATH)?;\n    let (mut sessions, mut users) = storage.load().unwrap_or_default();\n    for mut stream in listener.incoming().flatten() {\n        let _ = handle(&mut stream, &mut sessions, &mut users, &mut storage);\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Route one fully read request.  Mutable in-memory state is saved immediately\n/// after every successful auth or combat mutation; relational domains commit in\n/// their storage methods.\nfn handle(\n    stream: &mut TcpStream,\n    sessions: &mut Sessions,\n    users: &mut Users,\n    storage: &mut Storage,\n) -> std::io::Result<()> {\n    let request = read_request(stream)?;\n    let Some(HttpRequest {\n        method,\n item_id)) = tail.split_once(\"/inventory/items/\") {\n                if !item_id.contains('/') {\n                    let Some((actor_name, _)) = authenticated_user(authorization.as_deref(), users)\n                    else {\n                        return respond(stream, 401, r#\"{\"error\":\"unauthorized\"}\"#);\n                    };\n                    return            200,\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if let Some((campaign_id, character_id)) = path\n            .strip_prefix(\"/v1/play/campaigns/\")\n            .and_then(|p| p.strip_suffix(\"/concentration\"))\n            .and_then(|p| p.split_once(\"/characters/\"))\n        {\n            let Some((actor_name, _)) = parse_play_quest_rewards(body: &str) -> Result<PlayQuestRewards, PlayQuestError> {\n    let obj = object(body).map_err(|_| PlayQuestError::Invalid)?;\n    let xp = number_field(&obj, \"xp\").map_err(|_| PlayQuestError::Invalid)?;\n    let items = as_object(field(&obj, \"items\").map_err(|_| PlayQuestError::Invalid)?).map_err(|_| PlayQuestError::Invalid)?;\n    if xp < 0 { return    Ok(PlayQuestRewards { xp, items: values })\n}\n\nfn configure_play_quest_rewards(campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, quest_id: &str, body: &str, storage: &Storage) -> Result<String, PlayQuestError> {\n
